8a6('bum): chen po la bslab par bya'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams la bslab par byi'o//de nas tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bus/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//
8a7('bum): bcom ldan 'das de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob pa'o//bsam gtan gyi pha 
8a8('bum): rol tu phyin pa la mi dmig pa'i tshul gyis slob po//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la/mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slab po//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la mid migs 
8b1('bum): pa'i tshul gyis slob po//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob po//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la ma de dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slobs po//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa 
8b2('bum): rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul kyis slob po//yang dag par spong ba rnams la mi dmigs pa'i chul gyis slob pa//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshil gyis slob po//dbang 
8b3('bum): po rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshal gyis slob po//stobs rnams la mi dmigs pa'i mtshal gyis slob po//byang chub kyi yan lag rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshol gyis slob po//'phags 
8b4('bum): pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la mi dmigs pa'i tshur gyis slob po//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams la mi dmigs ba'i tshul gyis slob po//bsam gtan rnams la mi dmigs pa'i
8b5('bum): tshul gyis slob po//tshad med pa rnams la mi dmigs la tshul gyis slob po//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la mi dmigs tshul gyis slob po//rnam par 
8b6('bum): thar pa rnams la mi dmigs bya'i tshul gyis slob po//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug ba rnams la mi dmigs ba'itshul gyis slob po//rnam bar thar ba'i sgo stong pa nyid
8b7('bum): dang|mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la mi dmigs pa'i 'tshul gyis slob po//mngon par shes pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob bo//ting nge 'dzin rnams la mi dmigs
8b8('bum): pa'i tshul gyis slob po//gzungs kyi sgo rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshal gyis slob po//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams la mi dmigs pa'i chul gyis slob po//mi
9a1('bum): @#//'jigs pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob po//so so yang dag par rig pa rnams la mi dmigs ba'i tshul gyis slob po//byams pa chen po la mi 
9a2('bum): dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob bo//snying rje chen po la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyas slob po//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams la mi dmigs dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/shA ra dwa ti'i phu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de ltar slob pa ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la me dmigs 
9a3('bum): pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mi dmigs pa'i tshul kyis slob pa yin/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mid dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slos 
9a4('bum): pa yin/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la mi mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyi slob pa yin/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis sleb pa yin/sbyin pa'i pha 
9a5('bum): rol tu phyin pa la mi dmig pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin /dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis/slob pa yin/yang dag par spong ba rnams la mi dmigs 
9a6('bum): pa'i tshul gyis sleb pa yin /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams la mi dmigs ba'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/dbang po rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshal gyis slob pa yin/stobs rnams 
9a7('bum): la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/byang chub kyi yan lag rnams la mi dmigs pa'i rgyu la gyes slob pa yin/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la mi dmigs 
9b1('bum): ba'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/'phags pa'i bden pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/bsam gtan rnams la mi dmigs pa'i rgyu la gyis slob pa yin/tshad med pa 
9b2('bum): rnams la mi dmigs pa'i chu la gyis slob pa yin/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la mi dmigs ba'i tshul gyis slob pa yin nam par thar pa rnams la mi dmigs
9b3('bum): ba'i tshul gyis slob pa yin|mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug parnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshol gyis slob pa yin/rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa 
9b4('bum): dang|smon pa med pa la mi dmigs pa'i tshul kyis slob pa yin/mngon par shes pa rnams la mi dmigs pa 'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/ting nge 'dzin rnams la mi dmigs pa'itshul gyis 
9b5('bum): slob pa yin/gzungs kyi sgo rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshal gyis slob pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshil gyis slob pa yin/mi 'jigs pa rnams 
9b6('bum): la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin/so so yang dag par rig pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyi slob pa yin/byams pa chen po la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis/slob pa 
9b7('bum): yin|snying rje chen po la mi dmigs pa 'i tshul kyis slob pa yin /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams la mi dmigasapa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bus gsol ba/
9b8('bum): bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du mi dmigs pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/shA ra dwa ti'i bu shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir bdag ma dmigs so/sems can mi 
10a1('bum): @#//dmigs so//'gro ba mi dmigs so//gso ba mi dmigs so//skyes bu mi dmigs so//gang zag mi dmigs so//shed can mi dmigs so//shed bdag mi dmigs 
10a2('bum): so//byed pa po mi dmigs so//tshor ba po mi dmigs so//shes pa po mi dmigs so//mthong ba po mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/gzugs mi dmigs so//shin tu
10a3('bum): rnam par dag pa'i phyir/tshor ba mi dmigs so//shin du rnam par dag pa'i phyir 'du shes mi dmigs so//shin du nam par dag pa'i phyir/'du byed mi dmigs so//shin tun rnam par dag pa'i 
10a4('bum): phyir/rnam par shes pa mi dmigs so//shin turnam par dag pa'i phyir/mig mi dmigs so//shin tul rnam par dag pa'i phyir/rna ba mi dmigs so//shinatu rnam par dag pa'i phyir
10a5('bum): dmigs so//shin tu rnams par dag pa'i phyir/lce mi dmigs so//shin tuu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/lus mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyiryid mi dmigs so//shin
10a6('bum): rnam par dag pa'i phyir/gzugs mi dmigs so/shin tu rnam par dag pa'iphyir/sgra mi dmigs so//shin tum rnam par dag pa'i phyir/dri mi dmigs so//shin tuM rnam par dag pa'i phyir
10a7('bum): ro mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/reg mi dmigs so//shin tru rnam par dag pa'i phyir chos mi dmigs so//shin rtu rnam par dag pa'i phyiramig gi khams mi dmigs
10a8('bum): so//shin tu rnam par dab pa'i phyir/gzugs kyi khams mi dmigs so//shin du rnam par dag pa'i phyir/mi gi rnam par shes pa'i khams mi dmigs so//shen tu rnam par dag
10b1('bum): pa'i phyir/rna ba'i khams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/sgra'i khams mi dmigs so/shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phy-ir/rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams mi dmigs so//shin 
10b2('bum): tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir /sna'i khams mi dmigs so/shan tu rnam par dag pa'i pyir/dri'i khams mi dmigs so/shen tu rnam par dag pa'i th+yir/sna'i rnam par shes pa'i khams
10b3('bum): dmigs so//shin tu rnams par dag pa'i phy-ir/lce'i khams me dmigs so//shin tu rna bar dag pa'i phyir/ro'i khams mi dmigs so/phyin tu rnam par dag pa'i byar/lce'i rnam par
10b4('bum): shes pa'i khams mi dmigasaso//shin tu rnam par dgapa'i phyir/lus kyi khams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag ba'i phyir/reg gi khams mi dmigs so//shan tu rnam par
10b5('bum): dag pa'i phyir/lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i khams ma dmigs so//shin tu rnaM par dag pa'i phyir/yid kyi khams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/chos kyi khams mi
10b6('bum): dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dang ba'i phyir/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i kham sa mi dmigs so//shin tu rnamapar dag pa'i phyir/sa'i khams mi dmigs so/shin tu rnam par
10b7('bum): dag pa'i phy-ir/chu'i khams mi dmigs so/shan tu rnam par dag pa'i phy-ir/mi'i khams mi dmigs so/shen tu rnam par dag pa'i th+yir/rlung gi khams mi dmigs so//shen tu
10b8('bum): rnam par dag pa'i phy-ir /nam mkha'i khams mi dmigs so//shen tu rnam par dag pa'i pyir/rnam par shes pa'i khams mi dmigs so//shin du rnam par dag pa'i phyir /ma rig pa mi
11a1('bum): @#//dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/'du byed mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam bar dag pa'i phyir/rnam par shes pa'i khams mi dmigs so//shin du rnam 
11a2('bum): par dag pa'i phyir/ming dang gzugs mi dmigs so//shin tu dag pa'i phyir/skye mched drug mi dmigs so//shin turnam par dag pa'i phyir/reg pa mi dmigs so//shin 
11a3('bum): tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir /tshor ba mi dmigs so//shin tu nam bar dag pa'i phyir/sred pa mi dmigs so//shinatu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/len pa mi dmigs so//shinata rnam par 
11a4('bum): dag pa'i phyir/srid pa mi dmigs so//shintu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/skye ba mi dmigs so//shinatu ma bar dag pa'i phyir/rga shi mi dmigs so//shin tun rnam par dag pa'i phyir
11a5('bum): sdug bsngal mi dmigs so//shin tur rnam par dag pa'i phyir/kun 'byung ba mi dmigs so//shin ta rnam par dag pa'i phyir/'gog pa mi dmigs so//shi na tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir
11a6('bum): lam mi dmigs so//shin tum par dag pa'i phyir/'dod pa'i khams mi dmigs so//shin du rnam par dag pa'i phyir/gzugs kyi khams mi dmigs so//shan tu rnam par 
11a7('bum): dag pa'i phyir /gzugs med pa'i khams mi dmigs sa//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i yir/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa mi dmigs so//shing tu rnam par dag pa'i phyiro/tshul khrims
11a8('bum): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi dmi gas so//shin tu rnam par dgapa'i phyir/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa ma dmigs so//shin tu rnam bar dag pa'i phyir/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin 
11b1('bum): pa mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi dmigs so//shin du rnam par dag pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi dmi gas so//shin 
11b2('bum): tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir /dran pa nye bar gzhan ma rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu dag pa'i phyir/yang dag par spong ba rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyar/rdzu 
11b3('bum): 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams mi dmigs so//shan tu rnam par dag pa'i phar/dbang po rnams mi dmigs so//stan du rnam par dag pa'i phyar /stobs rnams mi dmigs so//shen tu rnam par 
11b4('bum): dag pa'i phyir/byang chub kyi yan lag rnams mi dmigs so/shin tu rnam par dag ba'i phyir/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa mi dmigs so//shinatu rnam par dag pa'i phyir
11b5('bum): ba'i bden pa rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag tha'i phyir/bsam gtan rnams mi dmigs so//zhin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/tshad med pa rnams mi dmigs so/shin tu rnam 
11b6('bum): par dag pa'i phyir/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams mi dmigs so/shan tu rnam par dag pa'iphyir/rnam par thar pa rnams mi dmigs so/shan tu rnam par dag pa' phyir/
11b7('bum): mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug ba rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyar/rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa mi dmigs 
11b8('bum): so||shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phar/mngon par shes pa rnams mi dmigs so/ /shin tu rnam par dag pa 'i phyir/ting nge 'dzin rnams mi dmigs so/shin tu rnam par dag pa'i pir/
12a1('bum): @#//gzungs kyi sgo rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams mi dmigs so//shing tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir 
12a2('bum): mi 'jigs pa rnams mi dmigs so//shi tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/so so yang dag par rig pa rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa'iphyir/byams pa chen po mi 
12a3('bum): dmigs so//shin tu rnamapar dag pa'i phyir/snying rje chen po mi dmigs so//shin tu dag pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams mi dmigs so//shin tu 
12a4('bum): rnam par dag pa'i phyir/rgyun tu zhugs pa mi dmigs so//shin tu nam par dag pa'i phyir/lan cig phyir 'ong ba mi dmigs so//shin tu rab par dag pa'i phyir/phyir mi 'ong ba mi 
12a5('bum): dmigs so//shin du rnam par dag pa'i phyir/dgra bcom pa mi dmigs so//shinatu rnam par dag pa'i phyir/rang sangs rgyas mi dmigs so//shin tu rnam par dag pa 'i phyir/
12a6('bum): byang chub sems dpa' mi dmigs so//shin tu ni rnam par dag pa'i phyir/byang chub mi dmigs so//shin du rnams par dag pa'i phyir/sangs rgyasami dmigs so//gsol pa/
12a7('bum): bcom ldan 'das/rnam par dag pa gang lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/chos thams cad ma skyes pa dang /mi 'gag pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /
12a8('bum): rnam par byang ba med pa dang /'byung ba med pa dang /dmigs pa med pa dang /mngon par 'du bya ba med pa 'di ni/rnam par dag pa zhes bya ba ste/shA ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar na/byang 
12b1('bum): chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos thams cad mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin no//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dbang 
12b2('bum): chen po chos gang dang gang la slob pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/shA ra dwa ti'i bu de ltar slob pa'i byang chubs sems dpa' sems dpa'-i chen po ni/chos gang la yang 
12b3('bum): slob pa med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/shA ra dwa ti'i bu chos 'di dag ni/byis pa so so'i skye bos ji ltar mngon par chags par bya ba de lta bu'i rnam pa yod pa ma yin no//gsol ba/bcom ldan 
12b4('bum): 'das 'o na chos 'di dag rnam pa gang gis mchis pa lags/bcom ldan 'da'as kyis bka' stsal pa/ji ltar med pa de ltar yod de/de ltar yod pa ma yin pas/de'i phyir med ces bya'o/
12b5('bum): gsol pa/bcom ldan 'da' ci'i slad du/mchis pa ma lags pa la/ma mchis pa zhes bgyi/bcom ldan 'das kyis bga' stsal pa/shA ra dwa ta'i bu gzugs nang stong pa nyid 
12b6('bum): dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'du sam byas stong pa 
12b7('bum): nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang/rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid 
12b8('bum): dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yod 
13a1('bum): @#//pa ma yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bu tshor pa nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang / stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam 
13a2('bum): pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'du ma byas stong pa nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa 
13a3('bum): stong pa nyid dang /rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid 
13a4('bum): dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang /dngos pa med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yod ma ma yin no//shA ra dA ti'i bu 'du shes nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyin nang stong pa 
13a5('bum): nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid de/chen po stong pa nyid dang don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dag 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang mtha' las 'das pa 
13a6('bum): stong pa nyid dang thog ma dang tha ma med par stong pa nyid dang /dor pa med pa stong pa nyid dang/rang bzhin stong pa nyid dag chos thams cad stong pa nyi dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa'i nyid dang /mi dmigs 
13a7('bum): pa stong pa nyid dang /dngosapa med pa stong pa nyid dag ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dag dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong ba nyid kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bu 'du byed nang stong pa nyid dang 
13a8('bum): phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid ston pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dag don dam pa stong pa nyid/'dus byas stong pa nyid do/'dus ma byas stong pa nid dang /
13b1('bum): mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang/rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid 
13b2('bum): dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang /dngosapo med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bu rnam par shes pa 
13b3('bum): nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'du sa ma byas stong pa 
13b4('bum): nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid dang /thog ma dang mtha ma med pa stong pa nyid gnyid dang /dor ba med ba stong pa nyid dang /rang bzhin stong pa nyidad dang /chos thams cadastong pa nyid dang /rang ga mtshan
13b5('bum): nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyidadang /dngos po med pa ma stong pa nyid dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dag /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stonang pa nyid kyi phyir yod pam yin no//shA ra dA ti'i 
13b6('bum): bu mig nang stong pa nyid dang phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyind stong pa nyid dang /stongapa nyid stong pa nyid dang/chen po stong pa nyid dang/don dam pa stong pa nyi dang /'dus byas stong ba nyid dang /'dus ma 
13b7('bum): byas stong pa nyid dang/mtha' las 'das pa stonang pa nyid dang /thog ma dngatha ma med pa stong pa nyidad dang/dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang /rangabazhin stong pa nyid dang/chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang 
13b8('bum): rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang/dngos po med pa stong ba nyid dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo ba nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yod ba ma yin no/
14a1('bum): @#//shA ra dwa ti'i bu rna ba nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus 
14a2('bum): byas stong pa nyid dang /'du ma byas stong pa nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /
14a3('bum): chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa'i ngo bo 
14a4('bum): nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bu sna nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi ma nang stong pa nyid dang //stong pa nyid stong pa nyi dang /chen po stong pa yin dang /don dam 
14a5('bum): pa stong pa nyid dang 'dus byas stong pa nyid dang 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid dang /thog ma dang za ma med pa stong pa nyid dag/dor ba med pa stong pa nyi dang /
14a6('bum): dang rang bzhin stong pa nyi dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyi dang /rang gi mtshan nying stong pa nyid dang /mi mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid gnyis dang /dngos po med pa'i stong pa nyid dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dag
14a7('bum): dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yid pa ma yin no//shA ri dwa ti'i bu la lu nang stong pa nyid dang phyi stong pa nyid dang phyi nang stong pa nyid dang stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang chen po stong pa 
14a8('bum): nyid dang don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dag/'dus ma byas stong pa nyi dang mtha' las 'das par stong pa nyid dang thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang dor ba med

27b6('bum): 'dus ma byas stong pa nyidadang /mtha' las 'das ba stong pa nyid dang thog ma dang tha ma med ba stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dag rang bzhin stong pa nyid do//chos thams cad stong ba nyid dang /rang
27b7('bum): gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dag mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dag/dngos po med pa stong ba nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dag/dngos bo med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong ba nyid kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no shA ra dwa ti'i bu mi 'jigs
27b8('bum): pa bzhi nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang phyi nang stong pa nyid dang stong ba nyid stong pa nyid ma chen po stong ba nyid dang don dam pa stog ba nyid dang 'dus byas stong pa nyid do//'dus ma byas stong ba
28a1('bum): @#//nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /ro ra ba med pa stong pa nyid dang/rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /
28a2('bum): rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no//shA ra 
28a3('bum): dwa ti'i bu so so yang dag par rig pa bzhin stong pa nyid dang phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas 
28a4('bum): stong pa nyid dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang /mtha' las 'dus pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa'i stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid/rang bzhin stong pa nyid/dang /chos thams 
28a5('bum): thams cad stong pa nyid dag rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa'i dang /mi dmigs pa ston pa nyid dang /dngos po'i med pa stong pa nyid dag ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nid stong pa nyid kyi phyir
28a6('bum): yod pa ma yin no//shAr dwa ti'i bu snying rje chen po nang stong pa nyid dang phyi stod pa nyid dang /phyi nang steng pa nyid dang /stong pa'i nyid stong pa nyid dang chen po stong pa nyid dang/don dam pa stong pa nyis dang /'dus 
28a7('bum): byas stong pa nyid dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid/mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang dor ba med pa stong pa nyis dang rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang chos thams 
28a8('bum): cad stong pa nyid dang rang gi mtshan nyid stong ba nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos pa med pa stong pa nyid dang/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang/dngos po med pa'i ngo bonyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir 
28b1('bum): yod pa ma yin no//shA ra dwa ti'i bu sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa 
28b2('bum): stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang rang bzhin stong pa 
28b3('bum): nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa 
28b4('bum): nyid kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no//de la byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams med pa la mngon par zhen pas/sred pa la mngon par zhen te/de dag gis med pa yongs su brtags pas/med pa la mngon par zhen cing 
28b5('bum): 'di lta ste/chad pa dang rtag pa'i mtha' la chags pas/de dag mi shes mi mthong ste/gang med pa'i chos de dag yongs su brtags nas ming dang gzugs la mngon par zhen pa de dag/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu 
28b6('bum): phyin pa la mngon par zhen to//chu la khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mngon par zhen no//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la mngos par zhen to//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par zhen to//
28b7('bum): bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mngo par zhen to//rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson par zhen to//nang stong pa nyid la mngon par zhen to/phyi stong pa nyid la mngon pa zhen to//phyi nang stong 
28b8('bum): pa nyid la mngon par zhenato//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid la mngon par/zhen to//chen po stong pa nyid la mngon pa'i zhen to//don dam pa stong pa nyid la mngon pas zhenato//'dus byas stong pa nyid la mngon 
29a1('bum): @#//par zhen to//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid la mngon par zhen to//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid la mngon parazhen to//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid la mngon bar zhen te 
29a2('bum): dor ba med pa stong pa nyid la mdon par zhen to//rang bzhin stong pa nyid la mngon pa zhen to//chos thams cad stong pa nyid la mngon par/zhen to//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid la mngon
29a3('bum): par zhen to//mi de migs pa steng pa nyid la mngon par zhin to//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid la mgon par zhen to//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la mngo par zhen to//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid 
29a4('bum): stong pa nyid la mngon parazhin to//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams la mngon par zhen to/yang dag par spong ba rnams la mngon par zhenato//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams la mngon par
29a5('bum): zhen to//dbang po rnams la mngon par zhe nato//stobs rnams la mngon par zhenate//byang chub kyi yan lag rnams la mngon par zhenata//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la
29a6('bum): mngon par zhen to//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams la mngon par zhin to/bsam gtan rnams la mngon par zhen//tshad med pa rnams la mngon par zhanato//gzugs med pa'i snyom+sa 
29a7('bum): par 'jug pa rnams la mngon bar zhen to//rnam par thar pa rnams la mngon par shen to//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la mdon par zhen to//stong pa nyid dang /
29a8('bum): mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la mngon par zhenato/mngon par shes pa rnams la mngon par zhes to//ting nge 'dzin rnams la mngon par zhed to//gzungs kyi sgo rnams la mngon
29b1('bum): par zhen to//snying rje chen po la mngon par zhen to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams la mngon par zhen to/mi 'jigs pa rnams la mngon par zhen no//so so yang dag par rig pa rnams 
29b2('bum): la mngon par zhen to//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams la mngon par zhan to//de dag de ltar mngon par zhen pas/yod pa ma yin pa'i chos rnams la yongs su rtog ste/de dag mi shes 
29b3('bum): mi mthong ngo //ci mi shes mi mthong zhe na gzugs mi shes mi mthong ngo //tshor ba mi shes mi mthong ngo /'du shes mi shes mi mthong ngo / 'du byed mi shes mi mthong ngo /rnam par shes pa mi shes 
29b4('bum): mi mthong ngo //mig mi shes mi mthong ngo //rna ba mi shes mi mthong ngo//sna mi shes mi mthong ngo /lce mi shes mi mthong ngo /lus mi shes mi mthong ngo / /yid mi shes mi mthong ngo /
29b5('bum): sgra mi shes mi mthong ngo /dri mi shes mi mthong ngo /ro mi shes mi mthong ngo /reg mi shes mi mthong ngo /chos mi shes mi mthong ngo /mig gi rnam par shes pa mi shes mi mthong ngo /rna ba'i 
29b6('bum): rnam par shes pa mi shes ma mthong ngo /sna'i rnam par shes pa mi shes ni mi mthong ngo /lce'i rnam par shes pa mi shes/lus kyi rnam par shes pa mi shis mi mthong ngo /yid 
29b7('bum): kyi rnam par shes pa mi shes pa mi mthong ngo /mig gi 'dus te reg pa mi shes mi mthong ngo /ba'i 'dus te reg pa mi shes/rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa ni 'dus te reg pa mi shes ma mthong ngo//
29b8('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa mi shes pa mi mthong nga /lus kyi 'dus te reg pa mi shes me mthong ngo //yid kyi 'dus te reg pa mi shis mi mthong ngo//yig gi 'dus te reg nga'i rkyen kyis tshor ba mi shes mi 
30a1('bum): @#//mthong ngo//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba mi shes me mthong ngo//sna'i 'dus ta reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor pa mi shes mi mthong ngo//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen
30a2('bum): gyis tshor ba mi shes mi mthong ngo//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba mi shes mi mthod do//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyed gyis tshor ba mi shes mi ma thong ngo//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa
30a3('bum): mi shes mi mthong ngo//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi shes mi mthong ngo//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa mi she mi mthong ngo//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yi shes mi mthong ngo//bsam
30a4('bum): gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi shas mi mthong ngo//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi shis mi mthong ngo//nang stong pa nyid mi shes mi mthong ngo/phyi stong pa nyid mi shes mi 'thong ngo//phyi nang stong pa nyid mi she
30a5('bum): mi mthong ngo//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid mi shes me mthong ngo/chen po stong pa nyid mi shes mi thong ngo//don dam pa stong pa nyid mi shes mi mthang ngo//'dus byas steng pa nyid mi shes mi mthod ngo//'dus
30a6('bum): ma byas stong pa nyid mi shes mi thang ngo//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid mi shes ma mthong ngo//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid mi shes pa mi mthong ngo//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid mi shis mi mthong ngo//
30a7('bum): rang bzhin stong pa nyid mi shes mi ma thang ngo//tshas thams cad stong pa nyid mi shes mi bzod ngo//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid mi she mi mthong ngo//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid mi she mi ma thong ngo//dngos
30a8('bum): po med pa stong pa nyid mi shas mi mthong ngo//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid mi shes mi mong ngo//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid mi shis ma mthong ngo//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi mi shes mi mthong ngo//yang
30b1('bum): dag par spong ba bzhi mi shes mi mthong ngo //rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi mi shes mi mthong ngo//dbang po lnga mi shes mi mthong ngo//stobs lnga mi shes mi mthong ngo /khyad chub kyi yan lag bdun mi shes mi mthong ngo//
30b2('bum): 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa mi shes mi mthong ngo//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams mi shes mi mthong ngo//bsam gtan rnams mi shes mi mthong ngo /tshad med pa rnams mi shes mi mthod do//gzugs 
30b3('bum): med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi mi shes mi mthong do//rnam par thar pa brgyad mi shes mi mthong ngo/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu mi shes mi mthong ngo/stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma 
30b4('bum): med pa dang /smon pa med pa mi shes mi mthong ngo /mngon par shes pa rnams mi shes mi mthong/ting nge 'dzin rnams mi shes mi mthong /gzungs kyi sgo rnams mi shes mi mthong do/snying rje chen po ma shes 
30b5('bum): mi mthong ngo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu mi shes mi mthong ngo/mi 'jigs pa bzhi mi shes mi mthong ngo/so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi mi shes mi mthod do/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa 
30b6('bum): rnams mi shes mi mthong ngo/
31a1('bum): @#//ches so//'du byed 'du byed kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so//rnam par shes pa rnam par shes pas stong zhes bya bar yid mis so//mi ga mig gis stong zhes bya bar yid 
31a2('bum): mi ches so//rna ba rnams stong zhes bya bar yid ma ches so//sna snas stong zhes bya bar yid la ches so//lce lces stong zhes bya bar yid ni ches so//lus lus kyis stong zhes bya bar yin mi ches 
31a3('bum): so//yid yid kyis stong zhes bya bar yod mi ches so//gzugs gzugs kyis stong zhes bya bar yang mi ches so//sgra sgras stong zhes bya bar yid me ches so//dri dris stong zhes bya bar yid yid mi ches so//
31a4('bum): ro ros stong zhes bya bar yid mo ches so//reg reg gis stong zhes bya bar yang yid mi ches so//chos chos kyis stong zhes bya bar yed mi ches so//mig gi rnam par shes pa mig gi rnam par shes pas 
31a5('bum): stong zhes bya bar yid mi chos so//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa rna ba'i rnam par shes pas stong ces bya bar yid mi ches so//sna'i rnam par shes pa sna'i rnam par shes pas stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so 
31a6('bum): lce'i rnam par shes pa lce'i rnam par shes pas stong ste zhes bya bar yid mi ches//lus kyi rnam par shes pa lus kyi rnam par shes pas stong shes bya bar yid mi ches so/yid kyi rnam par shes 
31a7('bum): pa yid kyi rnam par shes pas stod zhes bya bar yid mi ches sa so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa mig gi 'dus te reg pas stong zhes bya bar ying mi ches so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa rna ba'i 'dus te reg 
31a8('bum): pas stong zhes bya bar yid ma ches so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa sna'i 'dus te reg pas stong /zhes bya bar yid ma ches sa so//lce'i 'dus te reg pa lce'i 'dus te reg pas stong ces bya bar yang mi ches 
31b1('bum): so//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa lus kyi 'dus te reg pas stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa yid kyi 'dus te reg pas stod zhes bya bar yid mi ches swo//mig gi 'dus te reg 
31b2('bum): pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba/mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bas stong zhes bya bar yid ni ches so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba/rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor bas stong 
31b3('bum): zhes bya bar yid mi ches do//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba/sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyed gyis tshor bas stong zhes bar yid mi ches so//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenakyis tshor ba/lce'i
31b4('bum): 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor bas stong zhes bya ba yid mi ches so//lus kyi 'dus te regs pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gos tshor bas stong zhes bya 
31b5('bum): bar yid mi ches so//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol du phyin pas stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa/bsam gtan gyi pha rolatu phyin
31b6('bum): pas stong zhes bya bar yidami ches so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyen pas stong zhes bya ba yid mchis so//nang stong pa nyid nang stong pa nyid kyis stong zhes bya bar yid
32a1('bum): @#//mi ches so//phyi stong pa nyid phyi stong pa nyid kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so//phyi nang stong pa nyid phyi nang stong pa nyid kyis stong zhes
32a2('bum): nyid stong pa nyid stong pa nyid kyis stong zhe bya bar yid mi ches so//chen po stong pa nyid chen po stong pa nyid kyis stong zhesabya bar yang mi ches so//don dam pa stong pa nyid don dam pa stong pa nyid
32a3('bum): stong shes bya bar yid mi ches so so//'dus byas stong pa nyid 'dus byas stong pa nyid kyis stong ces bya bar yid mi ches so
32a4('bum): yid mi ches so//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid kyis stong zhes bya ba yid mi ches so//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid thog ma dang tha ma med pastong pa nyid
32a5('bum): kyis stong zhes bya bar yi mi ches so//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dor ba med pa stong pa nyis kyis stong zhes bya bar yin mi ches so//rang bzhin stong pa nyid rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyis stong zhes bya bar
32a6('bum): yid mi ches so//chos thams cad stong pa nyid/chos thams cad stong pa nyis kyis stong zhe bya bar yid ma ches so//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid/rang gi mtshan nyid stong ba nyid kyis stong zhe bya bar
32a7('bum): yid mi ches so//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid kyis stong zhe bya bar yang mi ches so//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid/dngos po med pa stong ba nyid kyis ston zhes bya bar yid mi
32a8('bum): ches so//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyis stong zhesabya bar yid mi ches so//dngos po mad pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid/dngos po mad pa'i ngo bo nid stong pa nyid kyis ston zhes bya ba
32b1('bum): yid mi ches so//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so//yang dag par spong ba bzhi/yang dag par spong ba rnams kyis stong zhes bya par yid 
32b2('bum): mi ches so//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams kyis stong zhes byar bar yid mi ches so/dbang po ba dbang po rnams kyis stong zhes bya 'di mi ches so//stobs lnga stobs kyis stong 
32b3('bum): zhes bya bar yid mi ches swo//byang chub kyi yan lag bdun/byang chub mi yan lag rnams kyis stong zhes bya dang yid mi ches so//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa/'phags pa'i lam yan
32b4('bum): lag brgyad pas stong zhes bya bar yid ma ches so//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams 'phags pa'i bden pa rnam kyas stong zhes bya bar yid la ma ches so//bsam gtan rnams bsam gtan rnams 
32b5('bum): kyis stong zhes bya bar yidami ches so//tshad med pa rnams tshad med pa rnams kyis stong zhes bar yid mi ches so//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams/gzugs med pa'i snyoms 
32b6('bum): par 'jug pa rnams kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so/rnam par thar ba brgyad/rnam par thar ba brgyad kyis stong zhes bya bar yi mi ches so//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 
32b7('bum): 'jug pa dgu/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyims par 'jug pa dgus stong zhes bya bar yid yi ches so/rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa/
32b8('bum): rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong ba nyid dang /mtshan ma medapa dang /smon pa med pas stong zhes bya bar yid ma ches so//mngon par shes pa rnams mngon par shes pa rnams kyis stong zhes
33a1('bum): @#//bya bar yid mi ches so//ting nge 'dzin rnams/ting nge 'dzin rnams kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so/gzungs kyi sgo rnams gzungs kyi sgo rnams kyis stong zhes bya 
33a2('bum): bar yid mi ches so/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stong zhes bya bar yid mi chos so//mi 'jigs pa bzhi/mi 'jigs pa bzhis stong zhes bya bar 
33a3('bum): yid mi ches so//so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi/so so yang dag par rig ba bzhis stong zhes bya bar yid mches so//snying rje chen po snying rje chen pos stong zhes bya bar yid me ches so//sangs rgyas 
33a4('bum): kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad/sangs rgyas gyi chos ma 'dres pa bco ba rgyad kyis stong zhes bya bar ying mi ches so//rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu/rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras pus stong zhes bya bar 
33a5('bum): ying mi ches so//lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu/lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'gras bus stong zhes bya bar yid ches so//phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu/phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bus stong zhes bya bar 
33a6('bum): yid mches so//dgra bcom pa nyid dgra bcom pa nyid kyis stong zhes bya bar yang mi ches so//rang byang chub rang byang chub kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches so//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid lam gyi 
33a7('bum): rnam pa shes pa nyid kyis stong zhes byar yid mi ches so//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid kyis stong zhes bya ba yid mi ches so//de dag ni gnas pa ma yin pa ste/
33a8('bum): ji ltar gnas pa ma yin zhe na/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa ma yin/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gan sa pa ma yin/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa gnas pa ma yin/brtson 
33b1('bum): 'grus kye pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa ma yin/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnad pa ma yin/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnang pa ma yin/nang stong pa nyid la gnas pa ma yin/phyi stong 
33b2('bum): pa nyid la gnas pa ma yin /phyi nang stong pa nyid la gnas bam yin/stong pa nyid stong pa nyid la gnas pam yin/chen po stong pa nyid la gnas par ma yin/don dam pa stong pa nyid la gnas pa'i ma yin/'dus 
33b3('bum): byas stong pa nyid la gnas pa yin/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid la gnas ma yin no/mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid la gnas ni/thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid la gnas/ma yin/

170b3('bum): stong pa zhes bya bar yid de/dmigs pa'i tshul kyis stong pa nyid stong pa nyid kyang dmigs la/stong ba nyid kyis kyang rlom semasasu byed do//de shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyo ba'i tshe/rnam pa 
170b4('bum): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dag ldan pa'i yid la bya ba yod par/lce chen po stong pa zhes bya bar yidal byed de/dmigs pa'i tshil gyis chan po stong pa nyid kyang dmigasal/stong pa nyid kyis kyang/rlom sems su 
170b5('bum): byed do//de shes rab kyis pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid de ldan pa'i yid la bya ba med par/lce don dam pa stong pa zhes bya bar yid la byed de/dmigs pa'i tshul gyis

170b7('bum): lce 'dus byas stong pa zhes bya bar yid du byed de/dmigs pa'i tshu la gyis 'dus byas stong pa nyid kyang dmigs la/stong ba nyid kyis kyang rlom sems su byed do//de shes rang kyi pha rol tu phyin ba la spyod pa'i

192b3('bum): kyang rlom sems su byed de//de shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang ldan pa' yid la bya ba med par/lus kyi rnam par shes pa don dam pa stong pa zhes 
192b4('bum): bya bar yid la byed de//dmigs pa'i tshul gyis don dam pa stong pa nyid kyang dmigasal/stong pa nyid kyis kyangarlom sems su byed do/de shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/ram pa thams cad 
192b5('bum): mkhyen gnyid dang ldan pa'i yid lab bya ba med par/lus gyi rnam par shes pa 'dus byas stong pa zhes bya bar yidal byed de/dmigs pa'i tshul gyis 'dus byas stong pa nyid kyang dmigs
192b6('bum): stong pa nyid kyis kyang rlom sems su byed do//de shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tsho/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen gnyid dang ldan pa' yid lab bya ba med par lus kyi rnam par shes pa

192b8('bum): pa la spyod pa'i tshe/rnam pa thams cad mkhye na pa nyid dang ldan pa'i yid la bya ba med par/lus kyi rnam par shes pa mtha' las 'das pa stong pa zhes bya bar yid la byed de/dmigasapa'i tshul
